      </w:rPr>
      <w:t>The MATCH function in Excel is used to locate the position of a specific value within a row, column or array. Unlike VLOOKUP or HLOOKUP, the MATCH function does not return the actual data but instead provides the relative position of the value. It is case-insensitive and works seamlessly in both horizontal and vertical ranges.</w:t>
